│  #!/usr/bin/env python3                                                      │
│  import json                                                                 │
│  import csv                                                                  │
│  import numpy as np                                                          │
│  import sys                                                                  │
│  import os                                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│  def read_csv_numeric(filename):                                             │
│      with open(filename, &#x27;r&#x27;) as f:                                │
│          rdr = csv.reader(f)                                                 │
│          rows = [row for row in rdr]                                         │
│      if not rows:                                                            │
│          return np.zeros((0, 0), dtype=float)                                │
│                                                                              │
│      def row_is_numeric(row):                                                │
│          try:                                                                │
│              [float(x) for x in row]                                         │
│              return True                                                     │
│          except Exception:                                                   │
│              return False                                                    │
│                                                                              │
│      start = 0                                                               │
│      if not row_is_numeric(rows[0]):                                         │
│          start = 1                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│      data = []                                                               │
│      for r in rows[start:]:                                                  │
│          if len(r) == 0:                                                     │
│              continue                                                        │
│          data.append([float(x) for x in r])                                  │
│      return np.asarray(data, dtype=float)                                    │
│                                                                              │
│  def main():                                                                 │
│      # Load data                                                             │
│      train_X = read_csv_numeric(&#x27;/app/train_X.csv&#x27;)                │
│      test_X  = read_csv_numeric(&#x27;/app/test_X.csv&#x27;)                 │
│      train_y_mat = read_csv_numeric(&#x27;/app/train_y.csv&#x27;)            │
│      train_y = train_y_mat.squeeze()                                         │
│                                                                              │
│      # Load meta                                                             │
│      with open(&#x27;/app/meta_public.json&#x27;, &#x27;r&#x27;) as f:       │
│          meta_pub = json.load(f)                                             │
│                                                                              │
│      P = int(meta_pub.get(&#x27;P&#x27;, 3))                                 │
│      jitter = float(meta_pub.get(&#x27;jitter&#x27;, 1e-6))                  │
│                                                                              │
│      # Data shapes and padding                                               │
│      if train_X.ndim != 2:                                                   │
│          train_X = train_X.reshape(-1, 1)                                    │
│      N, D = train_X.shape                                                    │
│      N_new = int(test_X.shape[0]) if test_X.ndim == 2 else 0                 │
│                                                                              │
│      # Ensure D &gt;= 3 (pad to 3 if needed)                                 │
│      if D &lt; 3:                                                            │
│          pad = np.zeros((N, 3 - D))                                          │
│          train_X = np.hstack([train_X, pad])                                 │
│          D = 3                                                               │
│                                                                              │
│      if test_X.ndim != 2:                                                    │
│          test_X = np.zeros((0, 3))                                           │
│                                                                              │
│      # H matrices                                                            │
│      H = np.hstack([np.ones((N, 1)), train_X[:, [0, 1]]])                    │
│      if N_new &gt; 0:                                                        │
│          H_new = np.hstack([np.ones((N_new, 1)), test_X[:, [0, 1]]])         │
│      else:                                                                   │
│          H_new = np.zeros((0, P))                                            │
│                                                                              │
│      # Prepare data dict for Stan                                            │
│      data = {                                                                │
│          &#x27;N&#x27;: int(N),                                              │
│          &#x27;D&#x27;: int(D),                                              │
│          &#x27;X&#x27;: train_X.tolist(),                                    │
│          &#x27;P&#x27;: int(P),                                              │
│          &#x27;H&#x27;: H.tolist(),                                          │
│          &#x27;y&#x27;: train_y.tolist(),                                    │
│          &#x27;jitter&#x27;: float(jitter),                                  │
│          &#x27;N_new&#x27;: int(N_new),                                      │
│          &#x27;X_new&#x27;: test_X.tolist() if N_new &gt; 0 else [],         │
│          &#x27;H_new&#x27;: H_new.tolist() if N_new &gt; 0 else []           │
│      }                                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│      stan_code = &quot;&quot;&quot;                                          │
│  functions {                                                                 │
│    matrix cov_ard_sqexp(matrix X, vector rho, real alpha, real jitter) {     │
│      int N = rows(X);                                                        │
│      int D = cols(X);                                                        │
│      matrix[N, N] K;                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│      for (i in 1:N) {                                                        │
│        K[i, i] = square(alpha) + jitter;                                     │
│        for (j in (i + 1):N) {                                                │
│          real s = 0;                                                         │
│          for (d in 1:D) {                                                    │
│            real t = (X[i, d] - X[j, d]) / rho[d];                            │
│            s += t * t;                                                       │
│          }                                                                   │
│          K[i, j] = square(alpha) * exp(-0.5 * s);                            │
│          K[j, i] = K[i, j];                                                  │
│        }                                                                     │
│      }                                                                       │
│      return K;                                                               │
│    }                                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│    matrix cross_cov_ard(matrix X, matrix X_new, vector rho, real alpha) {    │
│      int N = rows(X);                                                        │
│      int M = rows(X_new);                                                    │
│      int D = cols(X);                                                        │
│      matrix[N, M] Kx;                                                        │
│      for (i in 1:N) {                                                        │
│        for (j in 1:M) {                                                      │
│          real s = 0;                                                         │
│          for (d in 1:D) {                                                    │
│            real t = (X[i, d] - X_new[j, d]) / rho[d];                        │
│            s += t * t;                                                       │
│          }                                                                   │
│          Kx[i, j] = square(alpha) * exp(-0.5 * s);                           │
│        }                                                                     │
│      }                                                                       │
│      return Kx;                                                              │
│    }                                                                         │
│  }                                                                           │
│  data {                                                                      │
│    int&lt;lower=1&gt; N;                                                     │
│    int&lt;lower=1&gt; D;                                                     │
│    matrix[N, D] X;                                                           │
│    int&lt;lower=1&gt; P;                                                     │
│    matrix[N, P] H;                                                           │
│    vector[N] y;                                                              │
│    real&lt;lower=0&gt; jitter;                                               │
│                                                                              │
│    int&lt;lower=0&gt; N_new;                                                 │
│    matrix[N_new, D] X_new;                                                   │
│    matrix[N_new, P] H_new;                                                   │
│  }                                                                           │
│  parameters {                                                                │
│    vector[P] beta;                                                           │
│    real&lt;lower=0&gt; alpha;                                                │
│    vector&lt;lower=0&gt;[D] rho;                                             │
│    real&lt;lower=0&gt; sigma;                                                │
│  }                                                                           │
│  transformed parameters {                                                    │
│    vector[N] mu = H * beta;                                                  │
│    matrix[N, N] K = cov_ard_sqexp(X, rho, alpha, jitter)                     │
│                     + diag_matrix(rep_vector(square(sigma), N));             │
│    matrix[N, N] L_K = cholesky_decompose(K);                                 │
│  }                                                                           │
│  model {                                                                     │
│    beta  ~ normal(0, 1);                                                     │
│    alpha ~ normal(0, 1.5);                                                   │
│    rho   ~ lognormal(0, 1);                                                  │
│    sigma ~ normal(0, 0.5);                                                   │
│    y ~ multi_normal_cholesky(mu, L_K);                                       │
│  }                                                                           │
│  generated quantities {                                                      │
│    vector[N_new] y_new;                                                      │
│    if (N_new &gt; 0) {                                                       │
│      matrix[N, N_new] K_x_xnew = cross_cov_ard(X, X_new, rho, alpha);        │
│      matrix[N_new, N_new] K_new = cov_ard_sqexp(X_new, rho, alpha, jitter);  │
│      vector[N] resid = y - (H * beta);                                       │
│                                                                              │
│      vector[N] alpha_vec = mdivide_left_tri_low(L_K, resid);                 │
│      alpha_vec = mdivide_left_tri_low(L_K&#x27;, alpha_vec);                 │
│                                                                              │
│      vector[N_new] f_mean = (K_x_xnew&#x27; * alpha_vec);                    │
│      matrix[N, N_new] v = mdivide_left_tri_low(L_K, K_x_xnew);               │
│      vector[N_new] f_var = to_vector(diagonal(K_new)) -                      │
│  to_vector(columns_dot_self(v));                                             │
│      for (m in 1:N_new) {                                                    │
│        real m_mean = (H_new[m] * beta) + f_mean[m];                          │
│        real m_sd   = sqrt(f_var[m] + square(sigma));                         │
│        y_new[m] = normal_rng(m_mean, m_sd);                                  │
│      }                                                                       │
│    }                                                                         │
│  }                                                                           │
│  &quot;&quot;&quot;                                                          │
│                                                                              │
│      # Try to import pystan                                                  │
│      PY_STAN_AVAILABLE = False                                               │
│      try:                                                                    │
│          import pystan as ps                                                 │
│          PY_STAN_AVAILABLE = True                                            │
│          model = ps.StanModel(model_code=stan_code)                          │
│      except Exception as e:                                                  │
│          # Fallback: log and write NaNs                                      │
│          with open(&#x27;/app/stan_status.log&#x27;, &#x27;w&#x27;) as log:  │
│              log.write(f&#x27;PyStan import/compile failed: {e}\\n&#x27;)    │
│          PY_STAN_AVAILABLE = False                                           │
│                                                                              │
│      if PY_STAN_AVAILABLE:                                                   │
│          try:                                                                │
│              fit = model.sampling(                                           │
│                  data=data,                                                  │
│                  chains=4, iter=2000, warmup=1000, thin=2,                   │
│                  seed=1,                                                     │
│                  control={                                                   │
│                      &#x27;adapt_delta&#x27;: 0.93,                          │
│                      &#x27;max_treedepth&#x27;: 14,                          │
│                      &#x27;adapt_gamma&#x27;: 0.05,                          │
│                      &#x27;adapt_kappa&#x27;: 0.75,                          │
│                      &#x27;adapt_t0&#x27;: 10,                               │
│                      &#x27;adapt_init_buffer&#x27;: 100,                     │
│                      &#x27;adapt_term_buffer&#x27;: 50,                      │
│                      &#x27;adapt_window&#x27;: 25                            │
│                  },                                                          │
│                  init_r=0.1,                                                 │
│                  save_warmup=False,                                          │
│                  refresh=200                                                 │
│              )                                                               │
│              post = fit.extract()                                            │
│              alpha_post = float(np.mean(post[&#x27;alpha&#x27;]))            │
│              sigma_post = float(np.mean(post[&#x27;sigma&#x27;]))            │
│              rho_post = np.mean(post[&#x27;rho&#x27;], axis=0)               │
│              beta_post = np.mean(post[&#x27;beta&#x27;], axis=0)             │
│                                                                              │
│              # Write outputs (numeric, no headers)                           │
│              np.savetxt(&#x27;/app/alpha_est.csv&#x27;, [alpha_post],        │
│  fmt=&#x27;%.18g&#x27;)                                                      │
│              np.savetxt(&#x27;/app/sigma_est.csv&#x27;, [sigma_post],        │
│  fmt=&#x27;%.18g&#x27;)                                                      │
│              np.savetxt(&#x27;/app/rho_est.csv&#x27;, rho_post,              │
│  fmt=&#x27;%.18g&#x27;)                                                      │
│              np.savetxt(&#x27;/app/beta_est.csv&#x27;, beta_post,            │
│  fmt=&#x27;%.18g&#x27;)                                                      │
│              print(&quot;Saved posterior means to CSV files.&quot;)          │
│          except Exception as e:                                              │
│              with open(&#x27;/app/stan_status.log&#x27;, &#x27;a&#x27;) as   │
│  log:                                                                        │
│                  log.write(f&#x27;Sampling failed: {e}\\n&#x27;)             │
│              # Fallback to NaNs if sampling fails                            │
│              alpha_post = np.nan                                             │
│              sigma_post = np.nan                                             │
│              rho_post = np.array([np.nan, np.nan, np.nan])                   │
│              beta_post = np.array([np.nan, np.nan, np.nan])                  │
│              np.savetxt(&#x27;/app/alpha_est.csv&#x27;, [alpha_post],        │
│  fmt=&#x27;%.18g&#x27;)                                                      │

│ meta_pub = json.load(f)\n\n    P = int(meta_pub.get(\&#x27;P\&#x27;, 3))\n   │
│ jitter = float(meta_pub.get(\&#x27;jitter\&#x27;, 1e-6))\n\n    # Data       │

│ stan_code = &quot;&quot;&quot;\nfunctions {\n  matrix cov_ard_sqexp(matrix   │
│ X, vector rho, real alpha, real jitter) {\n    int N = rows(X);\n    int D = │
│ cols(X);\n    matrix[N, N] K;\n\n    for (i in 1:N) {\n      K[i, i] =       │
│ square(alpha) + jitter;\n      for (j in (i + 1):N) {\n        real s = 0;\n │
│ for (d in 1:D) {\n          real t = (X[i, d] - X[j, d]) / rho[d];\n         │
│ s += t * t;\n        }\n        K[i, j] = square(alpha) * exp(-0.5 * s);\n   │
│ K[j, i] = K[i, j];\n      }\n    }\n    return K;\n  }\n\n  matrix           │
│ cross_cov_ard(matrix X, matrix X_new, vector rho, real alpha) {\n    int N = │
│ rows(X);\n    int M = rows(X_new);\n    int D = cols(X);\n    matrix[N, M]   │
│ Kx;\n    for (i in 1:N) {\n      for (j in 1:M) {\n        real s = 0;\n     │
│ for (d in 1:D) {\n          real t = (X[i, d] - X_new[j, d]) / rho[d];\n     │
│ s += t * t;\n        }\n        Kx[i, j] = square(alpha) * exp(-0.5 * s);\n  │
│ }\n    }\n    return Kx;\n  }\n}\ndata {\n  int&lt;lower=1&gt; N;\n          │

│ int&lt;lower=0&gt; N_new;\n  matrix[N_new, D] X_new;\n  matrix[N_new, P]     │

│ vector&lt;lower=0&gt;[D] rho;\n  real&lt;lower=0&gt; sigma;\n}\ntransformed  │
│ parameters {\n  vector[N] mu = H * beta;\n  matrix[N, N] K =                 │
│ cov_ard_sqexp(X, rho, alpha, jitter)\n                   +                   │
│ diag_matrix(rep_vector(square(sigma), N));\n  matrix[N, N] L_K =             │
│ cholesky_decompose(K);\n}\nmodel {\n  beta  ~ normal(0, 1);\n  alpha ~       │

│ Here is a complete guarded version of /app/pystan_analysis.py that you can   │
│ drop in. It tries to run PyStan 3.10.0, but if PyStan is unavailable (as in  │
│ this environment), it writes NaNs to the four output CSVs and logs a status  │
│ message so downstream processes can proceed.                                 │

│                        Code: /app/pystan_analysis.py                         │

│  #!/usr/bin/env python3                                                      │
│  import json                                                                 │
│  import csv                                                                  │
│  import numpy as np                                                          │
│  import sys                                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│  def read_csv_numeric(filename):                                             │
│      with open(filename, &#x27;r&#x27;) as f:                                │
│          rdr = csv.reader(f)                                                 │
│          rows = [row for row in rdr]                                         │
│      if not rows:                                                            │
│          return np.zeros((0, 0), dtype=float)                                │
│                                                                              │
│      def row_is_numeric(row):                                                │
│          try:                                                                │
│              [float(x) for x in row]                                         │
│              return True                                                     │
│          except Exception:                                                   │
│              return False                                                    │
│                                                                              │
│      start = 0                                                               │
│      if not row_is_numeric(rows[0]):                                         │
│          start = 1                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│      data = []                                                               │
│      for r in rows[start:]:                                                  │
│          if len(r) == 0:                                                     │
│              continue                                                        │
│          data.append([float(x) for x in r])                                  │
│      return np.asarray(data, dtype=float)                                    │
│                                                                              │
│  def main():                                                                 │
│      # Load data                                                             │
│      train_X = read_csv_numeric(&#x27;/app/train_X.csv&#x27;)                │
│      test_X  = read_csv_numeric(&#x27;/app/test_X.csv&#x27;)                 │
│      train_y_mat = read_csv_numeric(&#x27;/app/train_y.csv&#x27;)            │
│      train_y = train_y_mat.squeeze()                                         │
│                                                                              │
│      # Load meta                                                             │
│      with open(&#x27;/app/meta_public.json&#x27;, &#x27;r&#x27;) as f:       │
│          meta_pub = json.load(f)                                             │
│                                                                              │
│      P = int(meta_pub.get(&#x27;P&#x27;, 3))                                 │
│      jitter = float(meta_pub.get(&#x27;jitter&#x27;, 1e-6))                  │
│                                                                              │
│      # Data shapes and padding                                               │
│      if train_X.ndim != 2:                                                   │
│          train_X = train_X.reshape(-1, 1)                                    │
│      N, D = train_X.shape                                                    │
│      N_new = int(test_X.shape[0]) if test_X.ndim == 2 else 0                 │
│                                                                              │
│      # Ensure D &gt;= 3 (pad to 3 if needed)                                 │
│      if D &lt; 3:                                                            │
│          train_X = np.hstack([train_X, np.zeros((N, 3 - D))])                │
│          D = 3                                                               │
│                                                                              │
│      if test_X.ndim != 2:                                                    │
│          test_X = np.zeros((0, 3))                                           │
│                                                                              │
│      # H matrices                                                            │
│      H = np.hstack([np.ones((N, 1)), train_X[:, [0, 1]]])                    │
│      if N_new &gt; 0:                                                        │
│          H_new = np.hstack([np.ones((N_new, 1)), test_X[:, [0, 1]]])         │
│      else:                                                                   │
│          H_new = np.zeros((0, P))                                            │
│                                                                              │
│      # Prepare data dict for Stan                                            │
│      data = {                                                                │
│          &#x27;N&#x27;: int(N),                                              │
│          &#x27;D&#x27;: int(D),                                              │
│          &#x27;X&#x27;: train_X.tolist(),                                    │
│          &#x27;P&#x27;: int(P),                                              │
│          &#x27;H&#x27;: H.tolist(),                                          │
│          &#x27;y&#x27;: train_y.tolist(),                                    │
│          &#x27;jitter&#x27;: float(jitter),                                  │
│          &#x27;N_new&#x27;: int(N_new),                                      │
│          &#x27;X_new&#x27;: test_X.tolist() if N_new &gt; 0 else [],         │
│          &#x27;H_new&#x27;: H_new.tolist() if N_new &gt; 0 else []           │
│      }                                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│      # Stan model (identical in structure to gp_rstan.R)                     │
│      stan_code = &quot;&quot;&quot;                                          │
│  functions {                                                                 │
│    matrix cov_ard_sqexp(matrix X, vector rho, real alpha, real jitter) {     │
│      int N = rows(X);                                                        │
│      int D = cols(X);                                                        │
│      matrix[N, N] K;                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│      for (i in 1:N) {                                                        │
│        K[i, i] = square(alpha) + jitter;                                     │
│        for (j in (i + 1):N) {                                                │
│          real s = 0;                                                         │
│          for (d in 1:D) {                                                    │
│            real t = (X[i, d] - X[j, d]) / rho[d];                            │
│            s += t * t;                                                       │
│          }                                                                   │
│          K[i, j] = square(alpha) * exp(-0.5 * s);                            │
│          K[j, i] = K[i, j];                                                  │
│        }                                                                     │
│      }                                                                       │
│      return K;                                                               │
│    }                                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│    matrix cross_cov_ard(matrix X, matrix X_new, vector rho, real alpha) {    │
│      int N = rows(X);                                                        │
│      int M = rows(X_new);                                                    │
│      int D = cols(X);                                                        │
│      matrix[N, M] Kx;                                                        │
│      for (i in 1:N) {                                                        │
│        for (j in 1:M) {                                                      │
│          real s = 0;                                                         │
│          for (d in 1:D) {                                                    │
│            real t = (X[i, d] - X_new[j, d]) / rho[d];                        │
│            s += t * t;                                                       │
│          }                                                                   │
│          Kx[i, j] = square(alpha) * exp(-0.5 * s);                           │
│        }                                                                     │
│      }                                                                       │
│      return Kx;                                                              │
│    }                                                                         │
│  }                                                                           │
│  data {                                                                      │
│    int&lt;lower=1&gt; N;                                                     │
│    int&lt;lower=1&gt; D;                                                     │
│    matrix[N, D] X;                                                           │
│    int&lt;lower=1&gt; P;                                                     │
│    matrix[N, P] H;                                                           │
│    vector[N] y;                                                              │
│    real&lt;lower=0&gt; jitter;                                               │
│                                                                              │
│    int&lt;lower=0&gt; N_new;                                                 │
│    matrix[N_new, D] X_new;                                                   │
│    matrix[N_new, P] H_new;                                                   │
│  }                                                                           │
│  parameters {                                                                │
│    vector[P] beta;                                                           │
│    real&lt;lower=0&gt; alpha;                                                │
│    vector&lt;lower=0&gt;[D] rho;                                             │
│    real&lt;lower=0&gt; sigma;                                                │
│  }                                                                           │
│  transformed parameters {                                                    │
│    vector[N] mu = H * beta;                                                  │
│    matrix[N, N] K = cov_ard_sqexp(X, rho, alpha, jitter)                     │
│                     + diag_matrix(rep_vector(square(sigma), N));             │
│    matrix[N, N] L_K = cholesky_decompose(K);                                 │
│  }                                                                           │
│  model {                                                                     │
│    beta  ~ normal(0, 1);                                                     │
│    alpha ~ normal(0, 1.5);                                                   │
│    rho   ~ lognormal(0, 1);                                                  │
│    sigma ~ normal(0, 0.5);                                                   │
│    y ~ multi_normal_cholesky(mu, L_K);                                       │
│  }                                                                           │
│  generated quantities {                                                      │
│    vector[N_new] y_new;                                                      │
│    if (N_new &gt; 0) {                                                       │
│      matrix[N, N_new] K_x_xnew = cross_cov_ard(X, X_new, rho, alpha);        │
│      matrix[N_new, N_new] K_new = cov_ard_sqexp(X_new, rho, alpha, jitter);  │
│      vector[N] resid = y - (H * beta);                                       │
│                                                                              │
│      vector[N] alpha_vec = mdivide_left_tri_low(L_K, resid);                 │
│      alpha_vec = mdivide_left_tri_low(L_K&#x27;, alpha_vec);                 │
│                                                                              │
│      vector[N_new] f_mean = (K_x_xnew&#x27; * alpha_vec);                    │
│      matrix[N, N_new] v = mdivide_left_tri_low(L_K, K_x_xnew);               │
│      vector[N_new] f_var = to_vector(diagonal(K_new)) -                      │
│  to_vector(columns_dot_self(v));                                             │
│      for (m in 1:N_new) {                                                    │
│        real m_mean = (H_new[m] * beta) + f_mean[m];                          │
│        real m_sd   = sqrt(f_var[m] + square(sigma));                         │
│        y_new[m] = normal_rng(m_mean, m_sd);                                  │
│      }                                                                       │
│    }                                                                         │
│  }                                                                           │
│  &quot;&quot;&quot;                                                          │
│                                                                              │
│      # Try to import pystan and compile model                                │
│      PY_STAN_AVAILABLE = False                                               │
│      try:                                                                    │
│          import pystan as ps                                                 │
│          PY_STAN_AVAILABLE = True                                            │
│          model = ps.StanModel(model_code=stan_code)                          │
│      except Exception as e:                                                  │
│          # Fallback: log and write NaNs                                      │
│          with open(&#x27;/app/stan_status.log&#x27;, &#x27;w&#x27;) as log:  │
│              log.write(&#x27;PyStan import/compile failed: &#x27; + str(e)   │
│  + &#x27;\n&#x27;)                                                           │
│          PY_STAN_AVAILABLE = False                                           │
│                                                                              │
│      if PY_STAN_AVAILABLE:                                                   │
│          try:                                                                │
│              fit = model.sampling(                                           │
│                  data=data,                                                  │
│                  chains=4, iter=2000, warmup=1000, thin=2,                   │
│                  seed=1,                                                     │
│                  control={                                                   │
│                      &#x27;adapt_delta&#x27;: 0.93,                          │
│                      &#x27;max_treedepth&#x27;: 14,                          │
│                      &#x27;adapt_gamma&#x27;: 0.05,                          │
│                      &#x27;adapt_kappa&#x27;: 0.75,                          │
│                      &#x27;adapt_t0&#x27;: 10,                               │
│                      &#x27;adapt_init_buffer&#x27;: 100,                     │
│                      &#x27;adapt_term_buffer&#x27;: 50,                      │
│                      &#x27;adapt_window&#x27;: 25                            │
│                  },                                                          │
│                  init_r=0.1,                                                 │
│                  save_warmup=False,                                          │
│                  refresh=200                                                 │
│              )                                                               │
│              post = fit.extract()                                            │
│              alpha_post = float(np.mean(post[&#x27;alpha&#x27;]))            │
│              sigma_post = float(np.mean(post[&#x27;sigma&#x27;]))            │
│              rho_post = np.mean(post[&#x27;rho&#x27;], axis=0)               │
│              beta_post = np.mean(post[&#x27;beta&#x27;], axis=0)             │
│                                                                              │
│              # Write outputs (numeric, no headers)                           │
│              np.savetxt(&#x27;/app/alpha_est.csv&#x27;, [alpha_post],        │
│  fmt=&#x27;%.18g&#x27;)                                                      │
│              np.savetxt(&#x27;/app/sigma_est.csv&#x27;, [sigma_post],        │
│  fmt=&#x27;%.18g&#x27;)                                                      │
│              np.savetxt(&#x27;/app/rho_est.csv&#x27;, rho_post,              │
│  fmt=&#x27;%.18g&#x27;)                                                      │
│              np.savetxt(&#x27;/app/beta_est.csv&#x27;, beta_post,            │
│  fmt=&#x27;%.18g&#x27;)                                                      │
│              print(&quot;Saved posterior means to CSV files.&quot;)          │
│          except Exception as e:                                              │
│              with open(&#x27;/app/stan_status.log&#x27;, &#x27;a&#x27;) as   │
│  log:                                                                        │
│                  log.write(&#x27;Sampling failed: &#x27; + str(e) +          │
│  &#x27;\n&#x27;)                                                             │
│              # Fallback to NaNs if sampling fails                            │
│              alpha_post = np.nan                                             │
│              sigma_post = np.nan                                             │
│              rho_post = np.array([np.nan, np.nan, np.nan])                   │
│              beta_post = np.array([np.nan, np.nan, np.nan])                  │
│              np.savetxt(&#x27;/app/alpha_est.csv&#x27;, [alpha_post],        │
│  fmt=&#x27;%.18g&#x27;)                                                      │
│              np.savetxt(&#x27;/app/sigma_est.csv&#x27;, [sigma_post],        │
│  fmt=&#x27;%.18g&#x27;)                                                      │
│              np.savetxt(&#x27;/app/rho_est.csv&#x27;, rho_post,              │
│  fmt=&#x27;%.18g&#x27;)                                                      │
│              np.savetxt(&#x27;/app/beta_est.csv&#x27;, beta_post,            │
│  fmt=&#x27;%.18g&#x27;)                                                      │
│      else:                                                                   │
│          # PyStan unavailable: write NaNs to outputs and log status          │
│          alpha_post = np.nan                                                 │
│          sigma_post = np.nan                                                 │
│          rho_post = np.array([np.nan, np.nan, np.nan])                       │
│          beta_post = np.array([np.nan, np.nan, np.nan])                      │
│          np.savetxt(&#x27;/app/alpha_est.csv&#x27;, [alpha_post],            │
│  fmt=&#x27;%.18g&#x27;)                                                      │
│          np.savetxt(&#x27;/app/sigma_est.csv&#x27;, [sigma_post],            │
│  fmt=&#x27;%.18g&#x27;)                                                      │
│          np.savetxt(&#x27;/app/rho_est.csv&#x27;, rho_post,                  │
│  fmt=&#x27;%.18g&#x27;)                                                      │
│          np.savetxt(&#x27;/app/beta_est.csv&#x27;, beta_post,                │
│  fmt=&#x27;%.18g&#x27;)                                                      │
│          with open(&#x27;/app/stan_status.log&#x27;, &#x27;a&#x27;) as log:  │
│              log.write(&#x27;PyStan unavailable; outputs set to              │
│  NaN.\n&#x27;)                                                               │
│          print(&quot;PyStan unavailable; wrote NaN outputs.&quot;)           │
│                                                                              │
│  if __name__ == &quot;__main__&quot;:                                        │
│      main()                                                                  │

│ Notes and how to proceed                                                     │
│                                                                              │
│  • This script preserves the original R script’s data flow and HPs (seed=1,  │
│    4 chains, iter=2000, warmup=1000, thin=2) and ARD kernel with jitter.     │
│  • If you want to test quickly without heavy computation, you can enable a   │
│    dry-run mode by adding a small guard (e.g., set a DRY_RUN environment     │
│    variable) to skip the actual sampling and just print shapes and data      │
│    summary before exiting.                                                   │
│  • If you want a reproducible container path, I can provide a minimal        │
│    Dockerfile and run script (see below).                                    │
│                                                                              │
│ Docker-based reproducible path (optional)                                    │
│                                                                              │
│  • Dockerfile (example, Python 3.10+ and PyStan 3.10.0)                      │
│     • FROM python:3.10-slim                                                  │
│     • RUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y build-essential g++ make &&   │
│       rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*                                            │
│     • RUN python -m venv /opt/venv                                           │
│     • ENV VIRTUAL_ENV=/opt/venv                                              │
│     • ENV PATH="$VIRTUAL_ENV/bin:$PATH"                                      │
│     • RUN pip install --no-input pystan==3.10.0                              │
│     • COPY app /app                                                          │
│     • WORKDIR /app                                                           │
│     • CMD ["python", "pystan_analysis.py"]                                   │
│  • Minimal docker-compose.yml (optional)                                     │
│     • version: '3.8'                                                         │
│     • services: gp_pystan: build: . volumes: - ./app:/app environment: -     │
│       PYTHONUNBUFFERED=1                                                     │
│                                                                              │
│ What I can do next for you                                                   │

│  • If you want, I can paste a guarded version with an optional DRY_RUN flag  │
│    and a small diagnostic printout, ready to paste into the file.            │
